# Household hh_s9 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple_plus_one. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office, storage.

Residents:

- **Ines** (resident_1): retired; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.61); average timing (jitter scale 1.17); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.29); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: friends, music, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.94); average timing (jitter scale 1.04); often forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.36); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: meditation, movies, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Hana** (resident_3): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.76); punctual (jitter scale 0.56); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.94); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the office desk. Hobbies: movies, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Yuki.

## Day 5 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d5`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:30–16:50).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d5`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:38–21:23).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_3:d5`: Hana has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `running_late:resident_2`: Yuki is running late all day (0.73): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.37, hurriedness 0.62, distraction 0.50; Yuki energy 0.98, hurriedness 0.73, distraction 0.44; Hana energy 0.46, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.39

**Active causes**

## Day 9 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d9`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:31–16:01).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d9:looming`: Hana has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Yuki is distracted (0.77): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.83, hurriedness 0.56, distraction 0.65; Yuki energy 0.59, hurriedness 0.47, distraction 0.77; Hana energy 0.86, hurriedness 0.27, distraction 0.22

## Day 10 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d10`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:07–15:37).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d9:due_today`: Hana's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d9:relief`: Hana made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d10`: Ines is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (14:26–16:26).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Yuki is distracted (0.78): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.70, hurriedness 0.41, distraction 0.54; Yuki energy 0.75, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.78; Hana energy 0.63, hurriedness 0.49, distraction 0.11

## Day 14 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d14`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:47–20:32).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d14`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:59–20:44).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_2:d14`: Yuki can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:59–24:00).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d14`: Ines has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.06): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.07): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.07): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 19 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d19`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:03–15:41).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d19`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:20–20:05).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d19`: Ines can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:50–24:00).

## Day 22 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d22`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:47–16:59).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d22`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:10–19:55).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d22`: Hana can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:12–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d22`: Yuki is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (15:58–17:58).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 23 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d23`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:24–16:35).
- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d23`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:57–16:47).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d23`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:33–20:18).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d23`: Ines can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:38–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.01): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d25`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:07–16:37).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_3:d25`: Hana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:38–12:38).
- `distracted:resident_3`: Hana is distracted (0.82): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.62, hurriedness 0.46, distraction 0.47; Yuki energy 0.39, hurriedness 0.26, distraction 0.52; Hana energy 0.56, hurriedness 0.31, distraction 0.82

## Day 26 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d26`: Hana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:38–16:08).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d26`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:16–21:01).
- `distracted:resident_1`: Ines is distracted (0.73): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `distracted:resident_2`: Yuki is distracted (0.75): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.63, hurriedness 0.31, distraction 0.73; Yuki energy 0.66, hurriedness 0.24, distraction 0.75; Hana energy 0.44, hurriedness 0.39, distraction 0.60

## Day 29 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d29`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:08–16:27).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d29`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:12–15:42).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d29`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:32–20:17).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d29`: Ines can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:06–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.36, hurriedness 0.44, distraction 0.61; Yuki energy 0.72, hurriedness 0.49, distraction 0.45; Hana energy 0.83, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.12

## Day 31 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d31`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:01–17:17).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d31`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:00–19:45).
- `distracted:resident_1`: Ines is distracted (0.81): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `running_late:resident_1`: Ines is running late all day (0.72): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.58, hurriedness 0.72, distraction 0.81; Yuki energy 0.96, hurriedness 0.60, distraction 0.41; Hana energy 0.62, hurriedness 0.29, distraction 0.15
